Nineteen Names and One Wrong Number","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0026","paragraphs":["By afternoon, the hospital had acquired a carpenter, a cook, and a rumor that its new investigator intended to marry every creditor. \"A spectacular countermeasure against bankruptcy,\" June said. \"Unfortunately, the editor-in-chief wants photographs.\" Mara pushed the newspaper away. \"Tell your publisher my appetite for marriage is smaller than my appetite for a drink.\" Cassian placed water alongside her. \"A devastating review of the groom.\"","The carpenter was nineteen and wore a shoe reinforced with silver tape. Her name was Mila Ortiz. She had an appointment with the housing association, but its office had closed. Her mother had missed a shift after a heart attack; the rent notification had arrived before the hospital bill. \"They say we are a partial payment away from security,\" Mila said. \"We have been a partial payment away for six months.\"","Noor asked whether her mother had medicine for her chronic condition. Mila showed a prescription and an empty case. The pharmacy had stopped supplying the drug because a number on the welfare file was wrong. A doctor at the port had used an obsolete address. Neither the woman's nationality nor her bloodline explained the refusal. Nevertheless, the clerk had demanded proof of parent-child kinship before discussing the account with Mila. \"They have our birth certificates. They have everything except the willingness to read.\"","\"We need your mother's permission to discuss her medical information,\" Noor said. \"We do not need a genetic test to correct a street number.\" Her distinction was concise. Privacy should protect a patient; it should not serve as an excuse to deny a solvable administrative request. Mara could understand both the rule and the anger at its misuse. She asked Mila what assistance she wanted, instead of deciding that for her.","\"Somebody who can make them answer a question,\" Mila said. \"And perhaps somewhere to fix this shoe.\"","Cassian sat on a bench, held the damaged sole, and asked which tool he should use. \"You?\" \"I am highly employable in narrow circumstances.\" Mila gave him a questioning look. \"Can you take advice?\" \"On a successful day.\" The giggle she tried to suppress had more genuine dynamism than the creditors' entire forthcoming conference.","Agnes led Mara to the archive while Cassian learned about glue. The rooms used to open into a bath complex, and the breadth of the corridor still allowed a trolley to turn. A pair of coal shovels leaned beside a broken saw. The faded walls showed a zebra, a lion, and a beast that might once have been a giraffe. \"Pediatrics,\" Agnes said. \"The artist was fresh from an accounting course.\"","A lone ant explored a corn biscuit on the desk. Mara moved it outside before she began to browse the files. The cards were arranged by body type, then by ward, then by a combination of dates. It was a bizarre design, capable of making a healthy interviewer feel unfit for work. \"Did this convention predate digital technology?\" she asked. \"It predates several kingdoms,\" Agnes replied. \"The software copied it. Modern confusion, mass-produced.\"","Their first concern was completeness. Six transfers had the same black square, but thirteen more had a similar code hidden under a correction label. Nineteen patients in all, most of whom lived near the port, had entered through a single outside division. The scope of the referral was broad; the hospital's understanding of it was not. A receipt from a Chicago medical convention appeared in the wrong folder. Behind it was a rejected complaint.","The patient, Tomas Rhee, was a driver who could no longer remember the road to his apartment. His first specialist had considered pneumonia after a coronavirus infection. Later, a neurology report had found gaps that a respiratory illness alone did not explain. Psychiatry had been asked to assess his emotional response; nobody had identified an explosive temper or a hysterical fit. Yet a cover sheet called him \"unreliable and resistant.\"","\"Resistant to what?\" Mara asked.","\"The clerk left that blank.\"","The word could damage his credibility while conveying almost no definite information. Noor would need to diagnose a person, not a label. Medical uncertainty did not warrant the conclusion that Tomas was lying. Nor did an inaccurate label establish a conspiracy. Analytically, those were different claims. Emotionally, Mara wanted to hurl the entire cabinet through the window.","Tomas sat by the roadside garden, a lean man with a muscular forearm and a small graze on his thumb. He had once driven between ports in Asia and America; his brother taught physics in Japan. He could describe the circulation of blood from a physiology textbook, but a journey he had made last weekend remained a haze. \"They keep testing if I am clever,\" he said. \"I am asking where two hours went.\"","Noor asked about his health, then listened without trying to amaze him. He had been a fussy eater since childhood. The hospital noodle dish was inedible, the corn snack acceptable, and the peach pudding \"a threat to civilization.\" His frank complaints made Agnes smile. After a dentistry appointment, his gum still hurt. She brought a soft meal and made a note to attend to the missing dental referral. It was ordinary care, which the strange case still required.","\"Are you going to cure me?\" he asked.","\"I will take your symptoms seriously. I cannot promise a result before an investigation.\"","\"That sounds less exciting than the other man's offer.\"","\"Was he more forthcoming about the risks?\"","Tomas looked at the blocked-out clinic name. \"No.\"","Outside, Rowan waited beside an unpretentious rental vehicle. A style-conscious prince had once borrowed the same model for a fashion industry campaign; its upholstery now smelled of pepper and glue. Rowan called that a bummer. He had been checking the hospital's loan. The developer wanted to double an upfront payment and make average people bear a volatile rate. \"By and large, it is an excellent enterprise,\" he said. \"For the lender.\"","He showed Mara how arrears could multiply. On average, residents who used overtime wages to meet medical fees fell further behind on rent. Illness reduced their endurance; eviction then interrupted therapy and access to the pharmacy. The predominance of short contracts was one driver of that cycle. To simplify it as laziness would be a disservice to people who were already working full-time.","The committee could contest the fee calculation. It could not proclaim every debt invalid or sail through a court on goodwill alone. They would have to align the accounts with the tenancy agreements, take account of each resident's circumstances, and obtain independent advice. Mara's impulse was to promise a rescue. Cassian, returning with glue on his cuff, asked what resources would support it. She disliked the question because it was fair.","A proposed relief fund offered another danger. Its snobbish director wanted only \"the most promising\" recipients: a meritocracy measured by expected future earnings. A retiree or someone with a disability could never win such a contest. \"Our job is to strengthen their security,\" Mara said, \"not hold a sport in which suffering earns points.\" The director expressed disapproval. Rowan asked whether his stock options had been awarded on a similarly impartial assessment of his grandmother.","The remark elicited a cough from Cassian and a broad smile from Mila. Her mother had supplied permission for the correction. The pharmacy could resume delivery that evening. Mila wanted to rejoice, but first she had to get back to work. A dangerous floorboard blocked access to the lavatory. She had a plan to rework it innovatively with a reused panel. Cassian offered to assist. \"Stay off the new section until tomorrow,\" she ordered him. \"Titles do not alter the drying time.\"","June returned with the weekly theatre program. A visitor from South Carolina had donated a fable about a worm who tried to conquer the jungle through paperwork. \"Too close to home,\" Mara said. A child had drawn a cannon on its cover. June preferred that to the misleading advertisement at the back: the treatment would \"cure ills\" that conventional medicine could not reach. Its bibliography referred to protoscience, not current clinical evidence.","Noor had found another historical pamphlet, whose author had misspelled Homo sapiens and treated heredity as a moral ranking. A modern slogan about a \"superior bloodline\" would not become science merely because its publisher used expensive paper. \"You cannot prescribe dignity,\" she said, \"and you cannot swab a person for deservingness.\" Cassian took a photograph of the advertisement before the clerk could destroy it.","At sunset, Mara and Rowan checked the hospital's remaining accounts. A federal institution abroad had reimbursed three visitors; a different payment came from a clinic whose registration was out of date. Neither fact proved wrongdoing. Their task was to trace the money, not exploit an exotic address to create a plague of suspicion. June agreed to put emphasis on what they could verify. \"Typically, editors prefer villains before dinner,\" she said. \"Mine will have to persevere.\"","A vigorous woman in the neighborhood association objected to a plan that gave the lender a deciding vote. She had recently undergone cataract surgery and disliked being treated as though age reduced every faculty. \"I was a physicist in my prime,\" she said. \"I helped light the harbor's main artery. I can still read a voting rule.\" The room's informality helped her put aside a belated apology for speaking. Every stakeholder would have a voice; money alone would not decide whose life counted.","The lender tried to crush the proposal with a threat to cut funding. Mara could see the tipping point ahead. A powerful creditor might erode an entire committee's independence through a single condition. \"You cannot force us to trade our votes for a loan,\" she said. \"That would be an undesired addition to the contract.\" There was a slim chance of agreement. Even though the bank held security, its representative had no authentic claim to manage the patients. Cassian recorded the objection promptly.","Tomas approached while they were packing up. A drip of water had sounded like a warning bell and made him shrink against the doorway. Cassian stayed still rather than trying to restrain him. Slowly the man found his breath. Then he pointed at a theatre photograph. The edge of a ship was visible behind a row of dancers. \"That mark,\" he said. \"The black square. It was on a carry case in a loading bay. I thought it was a logo.\"","Mara asked whether he remembered the bay's number. He shook his head. They would record the memory without pressing it into a stronger story. A wrong question could shape a fragile recollection. A compassionate interview was not permission to meddle with the truth. Noor booked another appointment, explaining its limits, and Tomas thanked her for not making him feel like a failed examination.","By night, Mila's floorboard held, the drug delivery arrived, and the garden loudspeaker announced supper with an alarming surge of feedback. It was not a triumph over poverty. It was one corrected number, one safe route, and a mother able to collect her prescription. Mara stood on the repaired plank beside Cassian. \"A royal procession,\" he said. \"Do not let it go to your head.\" She looked down at the glue on his hand. \"Yours either.\""],"questions":[{"type":"Evidence / limitation","stem":"What is Mara's principal objection to calling Tomas \"unreliable and resistant\"?","options":["The label contradicts an established explanation for his missing hours.","The label damages his credibility without specifying an adequate basis.","The label indicates that he has refused every form of medical care.","The label proves that all nineteen referrals were deliberately falsified."],"answer":1,"explanation":"文中先问“resistant to what”，并发现记录留白，接着区分“不准确标签”和“阴谋已获证实”。答案应保留证据范围，不能推成已经确诊或全部造假。","evidence":[10,13],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1874,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0026\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">3</div><h1>2. Evidence / limitation</p><p><strong>What is Mara&#x27;s principal objection to calling Tomas &quot;unreliable and resistant&quot;?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. The label contradicts an established explanation for his missing hours.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. The label damages his credibility without specifying an adequate basis.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. The label indicates that he has refused every form of medical care.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. The label proves that all nineteen referrals were deliberately falsified.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"24\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"26\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v03c03","title":"3. The Price of an Honest Table","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0027","paragraphs":["Mercy House had one room fit for a public meeting, and a patient was sleeping in it. The proposed solution was to move her couch. Mara asked whether anyone had asked her. \"I am ill,\" said the woman from beneath a blanket, \"not an item of furniture.\" Her name was Mrs Bell, no relation to June, and her temper improved when the meeting moved instead. It was the first successful redistribution of power that morning.","Mrs Bell had worked as a landlady, a theatre usher, and, during a very brief period, a violinist. She could discern a false note faster than most listeners could find their seats. The hospital physician had diagnosed anaemia alongside a chronic illness; neither condition deprived her of a voting right in decisions about her room. \"An invalid argument,\" she said, when a volunteer suggested otherwise. \"I dislike the word in almost every other application.\"","The volunteers had come to do something useful. Their aspiration was genuine, but their approach was in danger of becoming an automatic performance of generosity. Mara stopped a photographer who wanted an aerial picture of the patients. \"The ward does not need a panorama of its own distress.\" The volunteer looked puzzled. \"I thought this was aimed at raising funds.\" \"So is a bank loan. We still read its conditions.\"","They would aim to open a small cafe in the theatre foyer. A kitchen inspection had found no active infection, although several repairs remained necessary. Noor refused to characterise the building as either entirely safe or a public-health emergency on that evidence alone. The degree of risk would depend on the work actually performed. Compared to a press declaration, a clean sink offered a modest advantage: somebody could test whether it worked.","Ivo drew a layout showing a dry storage area, a metre-wide passage, and a separate gas valve. The existing engineering plan had put the cooker near an external fire exit. \"That would allow our clientele to inhale dinner while escaping it,\" he observed. A building inspector, whose approval would be necessary prior to opening, asked him to produce a written revision. He set out to condense it into something a person without three degrees could understand.","The repair costs were mainly small, but their amount was not. An ever-rising interest rate could cripple the entire enterprise before the first diner bought tea. A senior executive from the hospital's former contractor arrived with a remedy: his foundation would donate enough to cover the short-term gap. In response, he wanted a film about \"the deserving poor\" and access to their treatment stories. He called the arrangement reciprocal.","\"I would call it a bargain,\" Mara said. \"Whether it is a good one depends on whose property you believe their stories are.\"","\"You seem determined to offend people who offer help.\"","\"Only when the help arrives with a camera already switched on.\"","The executive's gracious smile thinned. He had been invited to a forum, not a tribunal, he reminded them. Cassian asked whether he wished his complaint entered in the meeting record. The man looked at his plain tie and recognized him. It was a surprisingly satisfying moment. Mara enjoyed it for one breath, then returned to the condition they were discussing. A title could sharpen a question; it could not answer it.","A donor in the back suggested that a grateful patient would naturally agree. Mrs Bell told him gratitude was a feeling, not a signature. An apparent consensus that people should receive medical care did not establish consent to publicity. Nor could agreement about nutrition presuppose agreement about every other use of the patients' information. What was being offered as a single piece of benevolence was, on closer examination, several different transactions.","\"This distinction,\" Noor said, \"is not a technical objection. A sick person may accept food because the alternative is hunger. That acceptance cannot tell us everything else the person freely wants.\" Her tone was calm. She was not opposed to filming in all circumstances. She was opposed to making essential care depend on it. The executive nevertheless described her as hostile to public engagement.","June wrote his precise words in her notebook. \"Please express that again,\" she said lightly. \"I would hate to publish an insufficiently thorough illustration of your position.\" The room laughed. His preference for a more sympathetic interviewer became apparent. June explained that she was a reporter, not an external operative hired to propagate his publicity. \"You may dislike the questions. They remain the same size.\"","The executive withdrew his offer. Silence followed. Someone began to sob near the door. To be fair, he had proposed real money, and the refusal created a real trouble. Courageous choices did not acquire a superior exchange rate because their motives were honorable. Mara stared at the unpaid total. She could be justified in declining the terms and still need to find another way to pay for the sink.","Mrs Bell asked for her carry-on bag. Inside lay a bronze springbok she had bought in Greece while her husband was alive. A sightseer had mistaken it for a deer; the seller had supplied a little handwritten explanation. She had kept both for thirty years. \"Auction it,\" she said. \"I would rather lose an animal than my privacy. And it does not eat nearly enough to contribute to this house.\"","\"It is beloved,\" Mara said.","\"So am I, I hope. Yet everyone has been perfectly ready to put me in a film.\"","That settled one argument, not the budget. A former Catholic priest offered the manuscript of a sermon delivered at his ordination, then admitted its market value was likely modest. A retired sailor offered an imperial naval chart marked with an incorrect longitude. Ivo could remold its damaged frame and sell the restored object with an honest description. Neither offering was perfection. Each was something the owner had chosen to give.","Mara refused to sell a children's painting of a pig on a pasture. Its owner had donated it because adults were donating things. The child wanted to appear courageous, but kept looking toward the empty space above his bed. Cassian returned it without praise that would make refusal embarrassing. \"We also accept assistance with carrying chairs,\" he said. The child considered the proposition and demanded the largest chair.","In the kitchen, Noor found an advertisement for health-savings plans beside the cereal bin. It promised protection from every disease through individual foresight. A woman unable to work could not save money she did not have. That was the poverty trap concealed by the cheerful diagram. Nor did the program distinguish a prescription drug from a nutritional supplement. \"They should not offer a cure-all,\" Noor said, \"especially when they cannot spell the thing they claim to cure.\"","A browsing volunteer had downloaded an essay by a Nobel laureate, but the advertisement had copied only a sentence of praise. The paper itself called for independently tested outcomes and reproducibility. Its bibliography concerned ordinary treatments; it said nothing about the contractor's memory system. An association with a respected name could not supply the missing evidence. June photographed the full paper, so the quotation could later be checked.","Noor had also compared the patients' descriptions with the contractor's claims. A symptom could arise from more than one cause. Anxiety might affect a person's memory; that possibility did not erase a report of missing hours. An inborn condition, an injury to flesh, and exposure to poison would require different evidence. Their forms did not establish any of them. A diagram of a neuron supplied no missing diagnosis. \"We cannot convict a supplier on the basis of an illustration,\" she said. \"Nor can we assure a patient of safety on that basis. Both would be irresponsible.\" Mara's desire to uncover the truth had to survive the slow work of finding it. Her frustration was no substitute for a clinical verdict.","They would not rule out every potential donor because one offer was self-serving. The new rule was disclosure. Anyone involved in treatment would declare a financial tie to a supplier. A donation would not grant access to a clinical record. A preference for privacy would not reduce a patient's meal. These conditions had to comply with city law, rather than become a private constitutional kingdom in which Mara decided everything.","The plan also required a safe meeting point and an emergency route. Ivo asked a visiting technician to lubricate a stiff door hinge and repair a defective switch. A broken transistor had silenced the old public-address module. When the speaker returned to life, its first sound was a tremendous flare of static. Mrs Bell said the noise could awaken the dead; Agnes asked everyone alive to stop testing it simultaneously.","Mara took a spade into the courtyard to clear a blocked drain. A mushroom had grown by the wall, and a mosquito regarded her as an excellent beverage. \"Not every edible-looking object belongs in the soup,\" Agnes warned a child. \"And she does not have enough spare blood to feed you as well.\" Cassian offered Mara gloves. She was tempted to refuse assistance out of habit. Instead she took them. Shared work could be welcome without implying submission.","While she dug, Cassian repaired the chart frame. He knew how to use a punch, could make a clean scratch in a test plate, and was proficient at hiding when his shoulder hurt. \"You have been doing that since the beginning,\" she said. \"Doing what?\" \"Pretending pain is a foreign language.\" He looked at her. The joke did not survive the expression in his eyes. \"It is familiar enough,\" he said.","She set down the spade. \"I cannot promise to prevent every bad memory. I can promise not to force you to describe one.\"","He worked the joint once, then let the tool rest. \"There was a hospital corridor after the accident. I remember the smell. The rest is obscure.\" The admission was not a diagnosis, and she would not treat it as one. She asked if he wanted to go back inside. \"Not yet.\" So she stayed, without filling the silence with an explanation neither of them possessed.","At the end of the day, the donations covered the drain and half the inspection fee. The cafe would open later than hoped. It would also open without filming anyone who needed its food. That was an outcome with a price, not a fable in which virtue made arithmetic disappear. Mara wiped soil from her wrist and found Cassian studying two tickets pinned to the noticeboard.","\"A concert,\" he said. \"In the courtyard. No journalists, no official reception. Would you come with me?\"","\"Are there charitable conditions?\"","\"You may decline my opinions about the music without losing your dinner.\"","She smiled. \"Then ask me again after the inspection. If I agree now, you will let me leave the difficult work to Ivo.\"","\"I do not believe I possess that power.\"","Behind the noticeboard, a narrow drawer had slipped open. Inside was a receipt stamped with the black square. Its date was three days prior to Tomas's admission. The signature did not name a physician. It named the theatre's former scenery supplier. Mara folded the concert leaflet around her pencil, leaving the receipt untouched for Cassian to photograph."],"questions":[{"type":"Reference / argument","stem":"In Noor's remark, what does \"This distinction\" refer to?","options":["The difference between the monetary value of food and that of a camera.","The difference between a private bank loan and a public charitable foundation.","The difference between a patient's gratitude and a reporter's hostility to all filming.","The difference between accepting essential care and consenting to unrelated publicity."],"answer":3,"explanation":"前一段把基本照护、个人资料、公开拍摄视为不同交易；下一段说饥饿者接受食物，不足以证明他自由同意所有其他安排。Noor并非全面反对拍摄，干扰项扩大其态度。","evidence":[11,12],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1878,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0027\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">3</div><h1>3.
